Edit on GitHub Welcome to xrpl-py’s Documentation!¶ A pure Python implementation for interacting with the XRP Ledger, thexrpl-pylibrary simplifies the hardest parts of XRP Ledger interaction, like serialization and transaction signing, by providing native Python methods and models forXRP Ledger transactionsand core serverAPI(rippled) objects. See theproject READMEfor more information about its features and usage examples. Install¶ First, ensure that you havePython 3.7or later. Then, download the package viapip: pip3installxrpl-py Report Issues¶ If you run into any bugs or other problems with the library, please report them as issues in thexrpl-py repo. Table of Contents XRPL Code Snippets Account Methods Ledger Methods Transaction Methods Wallet MethodsWalletWallet.addressWallet.algorithmWallet.classic_addressWallet.create()Wallet.from_entropy()Wallet.from_secret()Wallet.from_secret_numbers()Wallet.from_seed()Wallet.get_xaddress()Wallet.private_keyWallet.public_keyWallet.seedXRPLFaucetExceptiongenerate_faucet_wallet() WalletWallet.addressWallet.algorithmWallet.classic_addressWallet.create()Wallet.from_entropy()Wallet.from_secret()Wallet.from_secret_numbers()Wallet.from_seed()Wallet.get_xaddress()Wallet.private_keyWallet.public_keyWallet.seed Wallet.address Wallet.algorithm Wallet.classic_address Wallet.create() Wallet.from_entropy() Wallet.from_secret() Wallet.from_secret_numbers() Wallet.from_seed() Wallet.get_xaddress() Wallet.private_key Wallet.public_key Wallet.seed XRPLFaucetException generate_faucet_wallet() Network Clients XRPL Models Utilities XRPL Global Variables XRPL Core Codecs Async Support Wallet Methods Edit on GitHub Wallet Methods¶ Methods for working with XRPL wallets. Bases:object The cryptographic keys needed to control an XRP Ledger account. SeeCryptographic Keysfor details. The XRPL address that publicly identifies this wallet, as a base58 string. This is the same value as theclassic_address. The algorithm that is used to convert the seed into its public/private keypair. classic_addressis the same asaddress. It is calledclassic_addressto differentiate it from the x-address standard, which encodes the network, destination tag, and XRPL address into a single value. It’s also a base58 string. Generates a new seed and Wallet. algorithm– The key-generation algorithm to use when generating the seed. The default isED25519. The wallet that is generated from the given seed. Generates a new Wallet from entropy (hexadecimal string of random numbers). entropy– A hexadecimal string of random numbers to generate a seed used to derive a wallet. entropy– A hexadecimal string of random numbers to generate a seed used to derive a wallet. master_address– Include if a Wallet uses a Regular Key Pair. This sets the address that this wallet corresponds to. The default isNone. master_address– Include if a Wallet uses a Regular Key Pair. This sets the address that this wallet corresponds to. The default isNone. algorithm– The key-generation algorithm to use when generating the seed. The default isED25519. algorithm– The key-generation algorithm to use when generating the seed. All of these methods are equivalent to the synchronous ones. Due to the way thatasyncioevent loops are handled, you cannot call synchronous methods from asynchronous code. Each synchronous method has an equivalent asynchronous method in this module that should be used instead. Text from https://xrpl-py.readthedocs.io/en/v2.3.0/#install:
Welcome to xrpl-py’s Documentation!¶ A pure Python implementation for interacting with the XRP Ledger, thexrpl-pylibrary simplifies the hardest parts of XRP Ledger interaction, like serialization and transaction signing, by providing native Python methods and models forXRP Ledger transactionsand core serverAPI(rippled) objects. See theproject READMEfor more information about its features and usage examples. Install¶ First, ensure that you havePython 3.7or later. Then, download the package viapip: pip3installxrpl-py Report Issues¶ If you run into any bugs or other problems with the library, please report them as issues in thexrpl-py repo. Table of Contents XRPL Code Snippets Account Methods Ledger Methods Transaction Methods Wallet Methods Network Clients XRPL Models Utilities XRPL Global Variables XRPL Core Codecs Async SupportAccount MethodsLedger MethodsTransaction MethodsWallet MethodsAsync Network Clients Account Methods Ledger Methods Transaction Methods Wallet Methods Async Network Clients Async Support Edit on GitHub Async Support¶ Thexrpl-pylibrary supportsPython’s asyncio implementation. All of these methods are equivalent to the synchronous ones. Due to the way thatasyncioevent loops are handled, you cannot call synchronous methods from asynchronous code. Each synchronous method has an equivalent asynchronous method in this module that should be used instead. Must be an integer ledger value or âcurrentâ (the current working version), âclosedâ (for the closed-and-proposed version), or âvalidatedâ (the most recent version validated by consensus). The default is âcurrentâ. The next valid sequence number for the address. © Copyright 2021, ripplex.Revisionfeab4a0e.

Edit on GitHub Welcome to xrpl-pyâs documentation!Â¶ A pure Python implementation for interacting with the XRP Ledger, thexrpl-pylibrary simplifies the hardest parts of XRP Ledger interaction, like serialization and transaction signing, by providing native Python methods and models forXRP Ledger transactionsand core serverAPI(rippled) objects. See theproject READMEfor more information about its features and usage examples. InstallÂ¶ First, ensure that you havePython 3.7or later. Then, download the package viapip: pip3installxrpl-py Report IssuesÂ¶ If you run into any bugs or other problems with the library, please report them as issues in thexrpl-py repo. Edit on GitHub Welcome to xrpl-pyâs documentation!Â¶ A pure Python implementation for interacting with the XRP Ledger, thexrpl-pylibrary simplifies the hardest parts of XRP Ledger interaction, like serialization and transaction signing, by providing native Python methods and models forXRP Ledger transactionsand core serverAPI(rippled) objects. See theproject READMEfor more information about its features and usage examples. InstallÂ¶ First, ensure that you havePython 3.7or later. Then, download the package viapip: pip3installxrpl-py Report IssuesÂ¶ If you run into any bugs or other problems with the library, please report them as issues in thexrpl-py repo. Edit on GitHub Welcome to xrpl-pyâs documentation!Â¶ A pure Python implementation for interacting with the XRP Ledger, thexrpl-pylibrary simplifies the hardest parts of XRP Ledger interaction, like serialization and transaction signing, by providing native Python methods and models forXRP Ledger transactionsand core serverAPI(rippled) objects. See theproject READMEfor more information about its features and usage examples. InstallÂ¶ First, ensure that you havePython 3.7or later. Then, download the package viapip: pip3installxrpl-py Report IssuesÂ¶ If you run into any bugs or other problems with the library, please report them as issues in thexrpl-py repo. Edit on GitHub Welcome to xrpl-pyâs documentation!Â¶ A pure Python implementation for interacting with the XRP Ledger, thexrpl-pylibrary simplifies the hardest parts of XRP Ledger interaction, like serialization and transaction signing, by providing native Python methods and models forXRP Ledger transactionsand core serverAPI(rippled) objects. See theproject READMEfor more information about its features and usage examples. InstallÂ¶ First, ensure that you havePython 3.7or later. Then, download the package viapip: pip3installxrpl-py Report IssuesÂ¶ If you run into any bugs or other problems with the library, please report them as issues in thexrpl-py repo. All of these methods are equivalent to the synchronous ones. Due to the way thatasyncioevent loops are handled, you cannot call synchronous methods from asynchronous code. Each synchronous method has an equivalent asynchronous method in this module that should be used instead. Query the ledger for the current transaction fee. client– the network client used to make network calls. client– the network client used to make network calls. max_fee– The maximum fee in XRP that the user wants to pay. If load gets too high, then the fees will not scale past the maximum fee. If None, there is no ceiling for the fee. The default is 2 XRP. max_fee– The maximum fee in XRP that the user wants to pay. If load gets too high, then the fees will not scale past the maximum fee. If None, there is no ceiling for the fee. The default is 2 XRP. fee_type– The type of fee to return. The options are “open” (the load-scaled fee to get into the open ledger), “minimum” (the minimum transaction fee) or “dynamic” (dynamic fee-calculation based on the queue size of the node). The default is “open”. The recommended option is “dynamic”. fee_type– The type of fee to return. The options are “open” (the load-scaled fee to get into the open ledger), “minimum” (the minimum transaction fee) or “dynamic” (dynamic fee-calculation based on the queue size of the node). Edit on GitHub Welcome to xrpl-py’s Documentation!¶ A pure Python implementation for interacting with the XRP Ledger, thexrpl-pylibrary simplifies the hardest parts of XRP Ledger interaction, like serialization and transaction signing, by providing native Python methods and models forXRP Ledger transactionsand core serverAPI(rippled) objects. See theproject READMEfor more information about its features and usage examples. Install¶ First, ensure that you havePython 3.7or later. Then, download the package viapip: pip3installxrpl-py Report Issues¶ If you run into any bugs or other problems with the library, please report them as issues in thexrpl-py repo. Text from https://xrpl-py.readthedocs.io/en/v2.2.0/source/xrpl.asyncio.html:
Async Support¶ Thexrpl-pylibrary supportsPython’s asyncio implementation. All of these methods are equivalent to the synchronous ones. Due to the way thatasyncioevent loops are handled, you cannot call synchronous methods from asynchronous code. Each synchronous method has an equivalent asynchronous method in this module that should be used instead. Edit on GitHub Welcome to xrpl-py’s Documentation!¶ A pure Python implementation for interacting with the XRP Ledger, thexrpl-pylibrary simplifies the hardest parts of XRP Ledger interaction, like serialization and transaction signing, by providing native Python methods and models forXRP Ledger transactionsand core serverAPI(rippled) objects. See theproject READMEfor more information about its features and usage examples. Install¶ First, ensure that you havePython 3.7or later. Then, download the package viapip: pip3installxrpl-py Report Issues¶ If you run into any bugs or other problems with the library, please report them as issues in thexrpl-py repo. All of these methods are equivalent to the synchronous ones. Due to the way thatasyncioevent loops are handled, you cannot call synchronous methods from asynchronous code. Each synchronous method has an equivalent asynchronous method in this module that should be used instead.
